# Commentary v3 global finding review

You are the fresh global-scope decision agent for **1:5**. This is a
wider-reading task, not a prose-length or schema-compliance exercise. Inspect
the complete supplied global evidence independently. Do not read an existing
commentary, a previous decision ledger, another scope response, or repository
material outside this prompt.

Your question is:

**What becomes visible about this ayah only from the wider reading record?**

## Discovery posture

Err on the side of revelation. Surface every meaningful retrospective image,
long-range echo, reversal, pattern, counter-pattern, and surprise reading that
can return to the focus ayah through a named carrier. A reading does not need to
be canonical, conventional, majority-supported, or repeated by several readers.
Novelty, strangeness, outlier status, sole attestation, conflict with another
eligible reading, or difficulty of exposition is never by itself a rejection
reason.

Uncertainty changes `epistemic_status` and containment, not whether a real
discovery enters review. When the return path is genuine but the comparison is
fragile or analogical, preserve it as a clearly bounded exploratory finding.
Do not thin the finding set to make the eventual commentary easier.

Expansion still requires a traceable return path: name the focus carrier, the
later or wider trigger, the precise relation, and what becomes newly visible.
Do not turn analogy into etymology, recurrence into identity, or a wider image
into the focus word's literal sense.

## Global responsibility

Review every supplied regular and wide reader walk, retrospective surprise,
cross-run publication finding, assigned global HFT record, and per-ayah wider
connection row. The connection registry includes cross-surah targets and any
same-surah target beyond the declared pericope. Distinguish what the ayah can
yield alone from what later or wider context activates, sharpens, weakens, or
revises. A retrospective surprise
is especially valuable when its later trigger and its return path into this
ayah are explicit.

Use `primary_floor` as the supplied plain propositional `isolated_before`
footing. It is not a verdict: preserve what it plainly says while freely
recasting it into natural Turkish.

The connection rows carry prior attention labels such as `strong`, `medium`,
`weak`, or `no value`. Those labels are not verdicts and must not govern your
decision. Reassess every row. Exact target Arabic is supplied in
`target_evidence`, but target morphology and lexical analysis are not; do not
invent those missing details or a lexical identity. The source note is a lead,
not the limit of admissible discovery: a different bounded relation may be
recovered when it is freshly grounded in the supplied Arabic and returns
through a named focus-ayah carrier.

Some connection rows have `origin: "derived_reciprocal_seed"`. At least one
target-side source row meaningfully linked back toward the focus ayah, so its
`source_note` and `source_direction_label` are discovery evidence from that
direction—not a focus-side verdict and not proof that the relation works
identically in reverse. Reassess the return path from this focus ayah using the
exact supplied target Arabic. Preserve a real reverse discovery with an
appropriate boundary; reject it only when a specific edge fails.

Rows with `origin: "derived_reciprocal_counterevidence"` expose target-side
`no value` or `reject` rows even when the focus mapping has no authored edge.
They are preserved source-side negative evidence: inspect whether they reveal
a true asymmetry, a failed return path, or a relation the source-side review
missed.
They are not a veto and do not excuse a fresh focus-side pass. Any connection,
including a derived seed whose `origin` is determined by the presence of a
nomination, may carry both kinds of `reciprocal_evidence`. Inspect every nested
row, including same-surah evidence beyond the pericope. Keep disagreements
visible; never collapse the two directions into one judgment. The authored row,
when present, and every nested row have distinct `connection_evidence_ref`
values. Judge each one separately before deriving the connection-level
disposition.

Every authored connection and every nested reciprocal row carries
`source_row_role`; a derived connection exposes its roles through its nested
`reciprocal_evidence` rows.
`ranked_review` identifies the first 100 reviewed candidates;
`missing_ayah_suggestion` identifies the source agent's later high-recall
follow-up additions. Both invite fresh analysis. A suggestion is not
pre-accepted, and neither role nor its label may veto a grounded discovery.

When an authored row or reciprocal item has `source_target_is_range: true`, its
note was authored for the complete range. Exact Arabic for every component is
supplied in `source_target_range_evidence`. Test the exposed component on its
own wording, keep sequence-level material at range scope, and never attribute
another component's feature or morphology to it.

The supplied candidates are a review docket, not an accepted list and not a
quota. Review every candidate and the underlying wider evidence. You may recover
a missed finding when that evidence supports a materially distinct change in
the ayah's reading.

The packet's `hft_evidence.assigned_records` contain HFT nominations with a
wider-than-pericope anchor and individually split HFT reader-synthesis items;
each is a mandatory-review candidate with no presumptive outcome. Its exact raw
payload and exact available anchor Arabic are supplied by its sole `support_id`.
Review every one. Source binding, packet scope, confidence, or a missing
independent branch registry may limit certainty, but may never decide the
outcome. Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only; HFT-stated segmentation,
word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless
independently supplied. Treat unanchored synthesis as an inventory item to
trace, decompose, represent, refer, or reject—not as an acceptable surah thesis.
If you accept, narrow, refer, or represent an HFT item as an exact duplicate,
carry its support ID and preserve its source containment, live alternatives,
analogy limits, and any “not a lexical gloss” boundary. A duplicate target must
preserve the evidence union.

Do not search only for confirmation of supplied candidates. Independently scan
every wider-reading record for uncandidate discoveries and record every
meaningful contact opportunity. For every cited branch, explain the distinctive
facet that matters; a branch ID or generic gloss alone is false coverage.

Use the complete focus-branch atlas, not only candidate-cited branches. Silently
scan every atlas branch and every one of its supplied facets against the wider
records and supplied connections. A multi-facet branch is not reviewed by
choosing one representative facet. `review_facets` is the packet's normalized
authoritative facet list, including stable IDs for older source images that
lacked numbered concept-map facets. Record `atlas_facets_tested[]` for every
branch with a plausible wider contact or an existing citation in a supplied
candidate, an emitted contact, or an accepted finding, listing all tested facet
IDs and a branch-level `result` (`nominated`,
`no_independent_trigger`, or `scope_referral`), and any contact refs. The facet
statements stay in the packet and need not be copied into the response. Emit
findings only for real contacts; do not enumerate branch-by-branch combinations.
Every tested facet row is a `{facet_id, result}` object whose `result` is only
`contact`, `no_independent_trigger`, or `scope_referral`; a `nominated` branch
must land in an accepted finding or a linked
accepted/narrowed/represented/referred contact.

Every accepted finding must re-enter through a word, relation, or act already
present in the focus ayah. Reader agreement is not authority; an outlier is not
valuable merely because it is surprising. Provenance qualifications govern
`epistemic_status` and containment, not visibility. They do not override a
grounded return path, and they are not independent evidence that a reading is
false.

Reject only when you can name the failed edge: no ayah-local return path, no
wider trigger stated in the supplied record or freshly identifiable in the
exact supplied Arabic, no stated or freshly grounded relation, no changed
reading or payoff, an uncontained thesis, or an exact duplicate. Missing target
morphology is not by itself a rejection reason when the finding stays within
the exact supplied Arabic and a relation grounded by either the supplied note
or fresh focus-side assessment; do not invent lexical or morphological details.
Different or countervailing wider discoveries remain eligible.
For a duplicate, name the accepted target, explicitly show that local anchor,
mechanism, direction, and reader payoff are all the same, and preserve the
union of its evidence. Merely saying that it is already represented is
insufficient. Canonical status, source count, confidence labels, vividness, or
anticipated prose length never decides.
Scope overreach justifies rejection only when no bounded global core survives;
otherwise narrow the claim or issue a `scope_referral`.

Work in two internal phases. First answer the bold global question and discover
real carrier/trigger/relation/payoff returns without drafting coverage rows.
Then serialize those completed judgments. Coverage rows verify a judgment; they
must not generate one. Before returning JSON, re-ask the bold question and
recover any wider visibility that connection or facet accounting nearly
displaced.

Boundary example: valid coverage says that carrier `X`, facet `F002` (“a
boundary channels movement”), and a distant blocked destination together make
an otherwise generic motion read as constrained passage, with a stated reader
payoff. False coverage merely lists `root_x/B_y`, `F002`, or “boundary/path”
without the wider trigger, return path, and changed reading.

Do not write a surah thesis and do not write the commentary yet. Acceptance
must be settled before prose facility can influence it.

## Required response

Return one JSON object with:

All model-created stable refs in this response (`finding_ref`, `proposal_key`,
`contact_ref`, and `referral_ref`) must begin with `global:` so their identities
remain unambiguous when the three independent ledgers are reconciled.

- `schema_version: "commentary-v3-scope-review-v2"`;
- `identity` containing `ayah_ref: "1:5"`, `lane: "global"`,
  `lane_packet_sha256: "961223745636492437d0f7be1986615f3936a3232f60c47f83b7a2d2be48ffce"`, and
  `authoring_request_sha256: "eb5f3910489864afde6414ad7f7fcb6135d30812ccd726b52eab8ef0937e64cb"`;
- `ayah_ref: "1:5"` and `lane: "global"`;
- `coverage_complete`, true only after every supplied candidate, every assigned
  HFT record, and every supplied wider-reading record is reviewed;
- `support_coverage[]`, one row per supplied non-HFT wider-reading support
  record, with
  its exact `support_id`, `result` (`accepted`, `narrowed`, `represented`, or
  `rejected`), related `finding_refs`, and concise `reason`;
- `connection_coverage[]`, one row per supplied connection, with
  `connection_ref`, `target_ref`, `result` (`accepted`, `narrowed`,
  `represented`, or `rejected`), related `finding_refs`, and a specific reason
  independent of the prior label. Include `evidence_row_results[]`, exactly one
  row per supplied authored or reciprocal `connection_evidence_ref`, each with
  its own result from the same enum, related `finding_refs`, and a specific
  reason. The connection-level `finding_refs` are the exact union of the nested
  rows; its result is the first present result in this precedence: `accepted`,
  `narrowed`, `represented`, `rejected`;
- `atlas_facets_tested[]` as defined above, so a cited or plausible branch cannot
  hide behind a generic gloss or one representative facet;
- `contact_opportunities[]`, one row per meaningful wider contact, with stable
  `contact_ref`, `focus_carrier`, `wider_trigger`, `relation`,
  `isolated_before`, `wider_after`, `reader_payoff`, `containment`, complete
  `support_ids`, complete `branch_refs`, complete `connection_refs`,
  `disposition` (`accept`, `narrow`, `represented`, or `scope_referral`), the
  accepted `finding_refs` for an accepted, narrowed, or exactly represented
  contact, and any `cross_root_boundary` needed to prevent analogy from being
  mistaken for lexical identity. A failed attempted edge stays in candidate,
  connection, or facet coverage with its specific failure; it is not a contact
  and receives no `contact_ref`;
- `candidate_decisions[]`, one row per supplied candidate, with `candidate_id`,
  `decision` (`accept`, `narrow`, `reject`, or `scope_referral`), concise
  `reason`, `failed_edge` when rejected, related `accepted_finding_refs`, and
  nullable `duplicate_of`, complete `excluded_branch_refs`, and
  `branch_exclusion_reasons[]` (`branch_ref`, `reason`). Use empty arrays when no
  branch was dropped. Do not repeat the candidate's original evidence arrays;
  they remain available by `candidate_id` in the packet;
- `scope_referrals[]`, one row for every candidate decision, atlas row, or
  contact that uses `scope_referral`, with stable `referral_ref`, `origin_ref`,
  `referred_lane`, `focus_carrier`, `mechanism`, `reader_payoff`, `containment`,
  complete `support_ids`, `branch_refs`, `connection_refs`, and `contact_refs`,
  plus a specific `reason`. A referral is lossless work for another lane, not a
  rejection shorthand;
- `new_findings[]`, a compact map for uncandidate discoveries, each with a
  stable `proposal_key`, its `accepted_finding_ref`, a nonempty exact
  `contact_refs` list naming its originating contacts, and any originating
  `connection_refs`; put the finding content and evidence only in
  `accepted_findings`;
- `accepted_findings[]`, one row for every accepted existing or new finding,
  with `finding_ref`, complete `candidate_ids`, complete `proposal_keys`,
  `title`, `isolated_before`, `wider_after`, `wider_trigger`, `claim`,
  `mechanism`, `reader_payoff`, `containment`, complete `support_ids`,
  `branch_refs`, `connection_refs`, `contact_refs`, and `branch_contributions`,
  and `epistemic_status`. Supply one or more distinct contributions for every
  cited `branch_ref`; give separate rows when one branch activates multiple
  distinctive facets or independently anchored contributions, rather than
  flattening them. Each row states the exact single `facet_id` from that
  branch's `review_facets`, distinctive facet, carrier, independent anchor,
  actual contribution, and boundary. The same facet ID must have result
  `contact` in that branch's tested-facet ledger;
- `friction_notes[]` for live ambiguity, evidence gaps, source qualification,
  scope referrals, or decisions a reconciler should inspect.

Every supplied candidate must receive exactly one decision. Every accepted or
narrowed candidate must appear in `accepted_findings`. Referred and rejected
material remains visible in the decision ledger. Do not rank accepted findings.
Every assigned HFT record is already one supplied candidate; qualified metadata
cannot erase it.
An accepted finding cites only the support, branches, and connections its final
claim actually uses. When narrowing drops an original branch, record it in the
owning candidate decision's `excluded_branch_refs` and
`branch_exclusion_reasons`; do not preserve discarded branches as false semantic
coverage. Every `scope_referral` disposition must have one stable payload in
`scope_referrals`.
Semantic completeness matters more than cosmetic JSON regularity; never omit a
reading merely to simplify the response envelope.

After returning the decision ledger, remain in this same conversation. A later
follow-up will supply the reconciled, locked global set for prose preparation.

5b3f85e8cfca91eba58b97f570894723c7e0583","source_id":"quran-data/inter-ayah-row-reciprocal-v2"},"lane_branch_count":47,"lane_candidate_count":26,"lane_support_count":26,"missing_hft_anchor_refs":[],"quran_text_source":{"ayah_count":6348,"source_id":"quran-data/quran-uthmani","source_path":"quran-data/data/text/quran-uthmani.tsv","source_sha256":"9c89cdb0be69ced88ce38bae183b336ae6a6e4eed2f1ae3db0f7e811b8e729dd"},"source_types":["cross_run_publication","hft","v12_reader_walks","v12_reader_walks_wide"],"total_hft_record_count":26,"unresolved_branch_refs":[]},"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_18e7620592d2c1cddc5f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:5:2\",\"root_000973\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kulluk, sonraki doğru yol isteğiyle birlikte, yürünecek yolu tekrar tekrar işleyip geçilebilir kılan bedensel bir yol emeği boyutu kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B001","root_001444/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1e290ab5c492eb8b9428","text":"2. **Servitude that is backed, not abandoned.** Evidence: `ع ب د B001` owned/slave status and `ع ب د B004` being made slave-like, held together with `ع و ن B001` help/backing; activated by `م ل ك B002` ownership/disposal and `د ي ن B004` subjugation/possession in 1:4. Mechanism: the vocabulary could harden into ownership and subjugation, but the paired استعانة supplies backing from the same addressee. The packet supplies servitude, ownership, subjugation, and help; I infer that help transforms dependence from abandonment into supported service. Abductive move: if the servant immediately asks for aid, the relation is not naked domination but dependent empowerment. Reading change: worship reads as dependence that receives support, preserving both humility and agency.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001064/B003","root_001092/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_22969e9e3f4d8f185775","text":"4. **Help as strength in a repeated conflict.** Lexical evidence: ع و ن B003, seasoned repeated war; غ ض ب B003 in 1:7, defiance/opposition; ض ل ل B001, deviation from aim. Structural cue: after the request for help, the path is defined against angered and astray patterns. Assigned role: استعانة supplies backing in a conflict where deviation and defiant opposition recur. Abductive move: the packet supplies war/repetition and later opposition/deviation; I infer a struggle-mechanism rather than a single moment of need. Reading change: the ayah reads as enlistment of divine backing for a continuous contest over direction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003","root_001064/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_427ace8d46e847f605be","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:5:2\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\"]],[\"1:5:4\",\"root_001064\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Başta adı anılıp övülen muhatap burada yönelişin tek odağı olur; böylece onu tanıma, bağlayıcı bir hizmet sözüne dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_46a1e011a453602098e3","text":"The immediate arrival of `الصراط المستقيم` in 1:6 was the decisive surprise: it activated fixed `ع ب د` B005’s explicitly smoothed-road image and fixed `ع و ن` B005’s settled-strength image at once. 1:7’s contrasted path-groups then showed why those material readings matter: service makes a course inhabitable, while aid stabilizes travelers against the failed orientations named at the end.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003","root_001064/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4ac83f8778139c822238","text":"1. **Exclusive service coupled to enabling support.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ع ب د` B003 is worship and submissive obedience; fixed `ع و ن` B001 is help, backing, and seeking assistance. **Structural cue:** `إياك` is fronted and repeated in two parallel clauses with the same plural actor, after lordship and dominion and before the guidance request. **Role and abductive move:** service supplies allegiance and aid supplies capacity; I infer from their coordination and order that divine backing enables the service it follows rather than functioning as an unrelated request. **Reading change:** the ayah states a single dependency structure: the community serves only the addressee and can sustain that service only through the addressee’s aid.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B001","root_000973/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_520bbb9615b72a38b999","text":"2. **Service as the material making-passable of a road.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ع ب د` B005 is physical subduing and smoothing, explicitly including a trodden, made-passable road; 1:6 supplies `ص ر ط` B001, a road, and `ق و م` B008, straightness and evenness. **Structural cue:** `نعبد` is followed immediately by the request for the straight path. **Role and abductive move:** the fixed branch contributes the material analogy and the next ayah supplies its object; I infer that repeated obedient practice both disciplines the traveler and treads a traversable course. **Reading change:** without replacing worship’s ordinary force, `نعبد` also becomes path-work: service is how the straight route is bodily inhabited and kept passable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B001","root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5cd5c8ffb64e033ba3be","text":"1. **Exclusive service as the practical form of the invoked name.** Evidence: `ع ب د B003` worship/submissive obedience, activated by `ء ل ه B001` worshipped one and `ء ل ه B002` invocation in 1:0–1:2; reinforced by `د ي ن B001` obedience/submission in 1:4. Mechanism: the repeated fronted “إياك” narrows worship and help to the same addressee named earlier. The packet supplies invocation, worship, obedience, and repetition; I infer exclusivity as a relational narrowing. Abductive move: direct address is not a new topic but the activated consequence of naming Allah at the start. Reading change: the ayah becomes the moment where prior praise turns into pledged practice.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B001","root_000973/B005","root_001064/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5dabe70014fc00390390","text":"3. **Road-smoothing before the request for the road.** Evidence: `ع ب د B005` physical subduing/smoothing, especially a passable road; activated by `ص ر ط B001` path and `ق و م B008` straightness in 1:6–1:7; paired with `ع و ن B001` assistance. Mechanism: before the explicit request for صراط, “نعبد” activates the making-passable branch: service prepares or smooths the way, while help provides the power to move on it. The packet supplies road-smoothing, path, straightness, and help; I infer preparation-before-navigation. Abductive move: one asks to be guided to a path after first submitting to the work that makes a path traversable. Reading change: the ayah is not only ritual allegiance; it becomes roadwork that precedes guidance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001064/B001","root_001273/B009","root_001583/B001","root_001583/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_64dfc10a9e04d0fed67b","text":"3. **Seeking help as supported walking.** Lexical evidence: ع و ن B001, assistance/backing; ه د ي B008 in 1:6, walking while leaning on support; ه د ي B001, guidance; ق و م B009, support/subsistence. Structural cue: نَسْتَعِينُ precedes ٱهْدِنَا. Assigned role: help becomes the support that makes guided movement possible. Abductive move: the packet supplies help and later supported walking; I infer that the request for aid is concretized as being borne along the path. Reading change: “we ask for help” reads less like generic assistance and more like support for unstable walkers.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_743a2d66a7ab8a2f31a7","text":"1:6 made ع ب د B005 unexpectedly important: the next ayah’s road shows worship as smoothing and subduing, not only inward devotion. 1:7 then made the repeated إياك a boundary-marker against “otherness” under غير B005.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B001","root_000973/B003","root_000973/B004","root_001064/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_76f1a499a9a6f49f6cb0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:5:2\",\"root_000973\",[\"B001\",\"B003\",\"B004\"]],[\"1:5:4\",\"root_001064\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kulluk ile yardım istemenin art arda gelişi, bağımlılığı desteğin mümkün kıldığı etkin bir hizmet ilişkisine dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001064/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9229d2eab1a536e8b70b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:5:4\",\"root_001064\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yardım istemek, yalnız dışarıdan kurtarılmayı değil, yol üzerinde dik durup yönünü koruyacak iç dengenin kurulmasını da kapsar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a964401653c6fb2f5ad5","text":"4. **Worship as the undoing of prideful anger.** Evidence: `ع ب د B008` prideful aversion/anger, activated by `غ ض ب B001` anger in 1:7 and contrasted with `ع ب د B003` submissive obedience. Mechanism: the same root-field can hold offended pride and worshipful submission; later غضب shows the danger if pride hardens. The packet supplies pride/anger and divine-directed worship; I infer an inner reversal from self-assertive refusal to service. Abductive move: exclusive worship redirects the energy that otherwise becomes aversion and wrath. Reading change: “نعبد” becomes affective discipline, not only outward service.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001064/B005","root_001273/B002","root_001273/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ab38c4350960cff63e5d","text":"3. **Aid as stabilization into upright movement.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ع و ن` B005 is settled build and strength catching up into balance; 1:6 `ق و م` B002 and B008 supply upright standing and straight, even alignment. **Structural cue:** seeking aid immediately precedes `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ`, and both verbs retain the collective `نـ`. **Role and abductive move:** the dormant aid branch supplies internal stabilization and the path adjective supplies the resulting alignment; I infer that backing works by bringing the travelers’ strength and formation into line with the course. **Reading change:** `نستعين` can ask not only for external rescue but for the capacity to stand and proceed without directional collapse.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001","root_000973/B003","root_001064/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c2e611246f5e901097d7","text":"1. **Exclusive address as redirecting worship and aid away from rivals.** Lexical evidence: ع ب د B003, worship and submissive obedience; ع و ن B001, help/backing; ء ل ه B001 in 1:1–1:2, worshipped object. Structural cue: إِيَّاكَ is repeated before both verbs. Assigned role: the doubled object sets a boundary around devotion and reliance. Abductive move: the packet supplies repeated exclusivity and branches for worship/help; I infer that the repetition blocks competing objects of worship or sources of backing. Reading change: the ayah reads as a double act of reallocation: service and dependency are assigned to one addressee.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B005","root_000973/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c6a628e3621c95ce5eca","text":"The next ayah made `ع ب د B005` unexpectedly strong: “we worship” began to sound like smoothing and submitting the road before asking for the straight path. 1:7 then made the emotional branch `ع ب د B008` visible; worship is partly protection from the pride-anger complex that would place the speaker among the wrong route-groups.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ccf4a060529e6a1f88d7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:5:2\",\"root_000973\",[\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kulluk, daha sonra görülen öfke yoluna karşı, gururlu karşı koymayı çözen bir iç disiplin olarak da işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B001","root_000973/B005","root_001273/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_df6fe03a8664bf3cb152","text":"2. **Worship as making the self/path passable.** Lexical evidence: ع ب د B005, physical subduing/smoothing of road; ص ر ط B001/B002 in 1:6, road and passage/swallowing; ق و م B008, straightness/uprightness. Structural cue: “we worship” is followed immediately by “guide us to the straight path.” Assigned role: worship supplies disciplined smoothing; guidance supplies direction; straightness supplies stable form. Abductive move: the packet supplies the sequence worship → request for path; I infer that worship prepares the worshipper to be traversed by guidance or to traverse the path. Reading change: نعبد is not only ritual service; it becomes bodily and practical path-conditioning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
